\llbracket{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\textsf{if}\;\underline{b}\;\textsf{then}\;\underline{x}\;\textsf{else}\;\underline{\textsf{iter}\{{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}x}\mid{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}y}\hookrightarrow{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\textsf{vblur}\,y}\}({\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}n})}}\rrbracket({\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\vec{b}}:{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\mathbb{R}^{2}},{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\vec{x}}:{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\mathbb{R}^{784}},\,{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\textsf{vblur}}:{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\textsf{Lin}}({\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\mathbb{R}^{784}},{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\mathbb{R}^{784}}),\,{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\vec{n}}:{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\mathbb{R}^{10}})

The program compiles to a
{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}4}
-linear map. Its inputs
({\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\vec{b}},{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\vec{x}},{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\textsf{vblur},{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\vec{n}}})
are determined by neural networks.

Model T
is similar to
Model D
. But compiles
\llbracket{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\textsf{if}\;\underline{b}\;\textsf{then}\;\underline{x}\;\textsf{else}\;\textsf{iter}\{{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}x}\mid{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}y}\hookrightarrow{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}y}\}({\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}n})}\rrbracket({\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\vec{b}},{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\vec{x}},{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\vec{n}})
and replaces its
{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}3}
-linear map with a random matrix
{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}W}
.

{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}W}({\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\vec{b}}\otimes{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\vec{x}}\otimes{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\vec{n}})

Model I
is the same model from the first experiment.

5.2.3.
Measures

The measures are unchanged from the first experiment.

5.2.4.
Results

The model training and data collection mirror the first experiment, using new training and test datasets (of same size) which specify the conditional and iterative image transformation.

Fig.
16
and
17
also mirror the analyses from the first experiment. The trends in the data are also similar. However,
Model D
now exhibits the best PSNR. Because it programs in the identity for when an input image is even, if the network can just learn to classify even digits, it will produce correct outputs on many test cases. This drives up its PSNR measure. The quality of its blurring is similar to the first experiment.

6.
Related Work

Cajal
({\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\multimap},{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\mathbb{2}},{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\mathbb{N}})
was deeply inspired by research in neurosymbolic programming, denotational semantics, and differentiable programming. We highlight their key insights while also taking the chance to identify where our work differs.

6.1.
Neurosymbolic programming

Neurosymbolic programs combine neural networks with discrete components. There are many approaches, but we focus on those which compile discrete components into differentiable form.

Scallop
,
TerpreT
, and
Differentiable Forth
are programming languages where discrete components are compiled into differentiable form
(
Li et al. 2023
;
Bosnjak et al. 2017
;
Gaunt et al. 2017
)
. Their programming paradigms differ—across logic, imperative, and probabilistic programming. Their programs blend neural networks solving perceptual tasks with discrete components performing logical tasks, mediated by the compiler as in
Cajal
({\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\multimap},{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\mathbb{2}},{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\mathbb{N}})
. These languages established that this kind of programming is possible for a rich array of tasks, and that it has positive impacts on learning dynamics. Our first intuitions on compiling discrete structure to differentiable form came from these languages.

However, they lack a theory of compiler correctness. No compiler correctness theorems and proofs are available for these languages. As a result, some principles underlying their design are difficult to understand or fix. For example, these languages lack first-class iteration. Before completing our work, it was unclear whether its absence was fundamental.
Cajal
({\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\multimap},{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\mathbb{2}},{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\mathbb{N}})
offers a simple setting to isolate and resolve these basic language design issues, not only for iteration but also other discrete structures (Section
7
).

6.2.
Denotational semantics

Linear types originate from linear logic, whose denotational semantics exposed a connection between linear programs and linear maps. These semantics span
decades
. Of special relevance are semantics which explicitly show the connection by computing denotations of programs as linear maps.

Our semantic approach is inspired by work from Valiron and Zdancewic, on categorical models of lambda calculi
(
Valiron and Zdancewic 2014
)
. They denote programs by linear maps between finite-dimensional vector spaces over finite fields, and their translation is proved sound and adequate. Several examples show programs with discrete structure denoted as matrices—though not over
{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\mathbb{R}}
-vector spaces. From these examples, we realized that if we could develop translations to
{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\mathbb{R}}
-vector spaces, then we could associate differentiable forms to discrete programs. But it was not clear whether this was possible. Fortunately, we encountered a denotational semantics of linear logic to Köthe sequence spaces
(
Ehrhard 2002
)
. These semantics denote programs by linear maps between
topological
vector spaces over
{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\mathbb{R}}
, notably including infinite-dimensional spaces.

However, their motivations differ substantially from ours. As a result, their translation inherits commitments that either prevent or complicate their use in our setting. For example, Köthe sequence spaces are more complicated than ordinary vector spaces; they are topological vector spaces. This is reasonable for their aims, but not necessary here.

6.3.
Differentiable programming

PyTorch
and
JAX
are differentiable programming languages widely used to build and train neural networks
(
Paszke et al. 2019
;
Bradbury et al. 2018
)
. Automatic differentiation in these languages frees programmers from manually implementing derivatives necessary for gradient-based learning in neural networks. They also make it easier to train neural networks at scale; they support specialized hardware which greatly accelerate training. There is growing interest in developing their programming language theory—especially on automatic differentiation
(
Abadi and Plotkin 2019
;
Elliott 2018
;
Krawiec et al. 2022
)
. Recent work introduced a new branch in this developing theory, on the prospect of first-class discrete structure in differentiable programming
(
Velez-Ginorio et al. 2026
)
. They show conditionals can be first-class, but defer on more complicated discrete structure.

With
Cajal
({\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\multimap},{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\mathbb{2}},{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\mathbb{N}})
, it is now clear iteration can be first-class.

7.
Discussion

Discrete structure is not
overtly
differentiable. However, compilers can translate discrete structure to differentiable form—broadening the class of differentiable algorithms we can express. Our work shows
Cajal
({\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\multimap},{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\mathbb{2}},{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\mathbb{N}})
can be used to develop these compilers. Its formal specification and metatheory are tractable, and yield a simple implementation for empirical studies on the interplay between learning and the discrete structures of ordinary programming. These efforts set up an exciting possibility, on whether algebraic data like lists and trees could be first-class in differentiable programming.

We suspect that these structures could be compiled to differentiable form by compiling to linear
recursive
neurons
(
Irsoy and Cardie 2014
)
. With recurrent neurons, their recurrent structure is simple and mirrors the natural numbers. But recursive neurons exhibit hierarchical recurrent structure—similar to the hierarchical structure found in algebraic data like trees. Establishing their formal correspondence would enable differentiable programming with first-class primitive recursion over algebraic data, a powerful programming abstraction. It is our next step.

Appendix A
Definitions

A.1.
Context Splitting

{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\Delta}={\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\Delta_{1}}\,\cup\,{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\Delta_{2}}

{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\varnothing}={\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\textsf{dom}}{({\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\Delta_{1}})}\,\cap\,{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\textsf{dom}}{({\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\Delta_{2}})}

{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\Delta}={\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\Delta_{1}}\circ{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\Delta_{2}}

A.2.
Length of a context

{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\textsf{len}}({\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\_}):{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\textsf{Context}}\to{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\mathbb{N}}

\displaystyle\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}{\displaystyle\textsf{len}}({\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\varnothing})={\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}0}

\displaystyle\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}{\displaystyle\textsf{len}}({\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\Delta,x\!:\!\tau})={\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}1}\,{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}+}\,{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\textsf{len}}({\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\Delta})

A.3.
Index set of context

{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\iota}({\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\_}):{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\textsf{Context}}\to\{{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\mathbb{N}}\}

{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\iota}({\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\Delta})=\{{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}1},...,{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\textsf{len}}({\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\Delta})\}

A.4.
Index of variable in context

{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\iota_{\_}}({\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\_}):{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\textsf{Var}}\times{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\textsf{Context}}\to{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\mathbb{N}}

\displaystyle\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}{\displaystyle\iota_{x}}({\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\varnothing})={\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}0}

\displaystyle\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}{\displaystyle\iota_{x}}({\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\Delta,y\!:\!\tau})=\begin{cases}\textsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}len}}({\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\Delta,y\!:\!\tau})&\textsf{ if }{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}x=y}\\
{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\iota_{x}}({\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\Delta})&\textsf{ otherwise}\end{cases}

A.5.
Context concatenation

{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\_\mid\mid\_}:{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\textsf{Context}}\times{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\textsf{Context}}\to{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\textsf{Context}}

\displaystyle\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}{\displaystyle\Delta_{1}}\,{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\mid\mid}\,({\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\varnothing})={\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\Delta_{1}}

\displaystyle\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}{\displaystyle\Delta_{1}}\,{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\mid\mid}\,({\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\Delta_{2},x\!:\!\tau})=({\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\Delta_{1}\mid\mid\Delta_{2}}),{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}x\!:\!\tau}

A.6.
Restricting a source environment

If
{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\sigma}\in{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\mathcal{R}}\llbracket{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\Delta_{1}}}\rrbracket
, then
({\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\sigma})_{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\Delta_{2}}
is the restriction of
{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\sigma}
to the domain of
{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\Delta_{2}}}
,

\displaystyle({\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\sigma})_{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\varnothing}={\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\varnothing}

\displaystyle({\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\sigma})_{{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\Delta_{2}},x:\tau}}=\begin{cases}({\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\sigma})_{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\Delta_{2}}{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\{x\mapsto\sigma(x)\}}&\textsf{if }{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}x}\in{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\textsf{dom}}({\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\sigma})\\
({\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\sigma})_{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\Delta_{2}}&\textsf{otherwise}\end{cases}

A.7.
Restricting a target environment

If
{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\vec{\sigma}}\in\llbracket{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\Delta_{1}}\rrbracket
and
{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\textsf{set}}({\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\Delta_{2}})\subseteq{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\textsf{set}}({\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\Delta_{1}})
, then
({\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\vec{\sigma}})^{{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\Delta_{1}}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\Delta_{2}}}\in\llbracket{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\Delta_{2}}\rrbracket
is the restructuring of
{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\vec{\sigma}}
according to
\llbracket{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\Delta_{2}}\rrbracket
. When the source context
{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\Delta_{1}}
is clear from context we use the notation
{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\vec{\sigma}}_{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\Delta_{2}}
as shorthand.

\displaystyle({\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\vec{\sigma}})^{{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\Delta_{1}}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\varnothing}}={\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\{0\}}

\displaystyle({\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\vec{\sigma}})^{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\Delta_{1}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\Delta_{2}},{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}x:\tau}}=({\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\vec{\sigma}})^{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\Delta_{1}}_{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\Delta_{2}}\,{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\times}\,{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\pi}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\iota_{x}}({\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\Delta_{1}})}({\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\vec{\sigma}})

Appendix B
Proofs

B.1.
Theorem (Programs evaluate to values)

{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\varnothing}\vdash{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}e}:{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\tau}\implies\exists{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}v},{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}e}\Downarrow{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}v}

Because programs can be logically typed
{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\varnothing}\vDash{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}e}:{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\tau}

By empty substitution and logical typing
{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\varnothing(e)}={\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}e}\in{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\mathcal{R}_{\textsf{E}}}\llbracket{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\tau}\rrbracket

Because logically typed programs terminate
\exists{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}v},{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}e}\Downarrow{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}v}

∎

B.2.
Lemma (Programs can be logically typed)

{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\Delta}\vdash{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}e}:{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\tau}\implies{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\Delta}\vDash{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}e}:{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\tau}

\triangleright

B.3.
Lemma (Logically typed programs evaluate to values)

Where
{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\sigma}\in{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\mathcal{R}}\llbracket{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\Delta}\rrbracket
,

{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\Delta}\vDash{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}e}:{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\tau}\implies\exists{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}v},{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\sigma(e)}\Downarrow{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}v}

By logical typing
{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\sigma(e)}\in{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\mathcal{R}_{\textsf{E}}}\llbracket{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\tau}\rrbracket

By logical relation
\exists{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}v},{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\sigma(e)}\Downarrow{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}v}

∎

B.4.
Theorem (Compiler preserves program behavior)

If
{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\varnothing}\vdash{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}e}:{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\textsf{base}}({\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\tau})}
, then

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} (a)\;&{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}e}\Downarrow{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}v}\implies\llbracket{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}e}\rrbracket=\llbracket{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}v}\rrbracket\\
(b)\;&{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}e}\;\cancel{\Downarrow}\;{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}v}\implies\llbracket{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}e}\rrbracket\neq\llbracket{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}v}\rrbracket\\
\end{aligned}

Consider each condition,

\triangleright

Condition

(a)
,

Because compiler preserves what programs will do
\llbracket{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}e}\rrbracket=\llbracket{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}v}\rrbracket

\triangleright

Condition

(b)
,

Because compiler preserves what programs won’t do
\llbracket{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}e}\rrbracket\neq\llbracket{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}v}\rrbracket

∎

B.5.
Lemma (Compiler preserves what programs will do)

{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\varnothing}\vdash{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}e}:{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\tau}\,\textsf{ and }\,{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}e}\Downarrow{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}v}\implies\llbracket{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}e}\rrbracket=\llbracket{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}v}\rrbracket

By induction on
{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}e}\Downarrow{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}v}
,

\triangleright

Case

{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}v}\Downarrow{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}v}

Because compiler is deterministic
\llbracket{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}v}\rrbracket=\llbracket{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}v}\rrbracket

\triangleright

Case

{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\textsf{succ}(e)}}\Downarrow{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\textsf{succ}(v)}}

By assumption
{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\textsf{succ}(e)}}:{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\mathbb{N}}

By inversion on typing
{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}e}:{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\mathbb{N}}

\displaystyle\llbracket{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\textsf{succ}(e)}\rrbracket

\displaystyle={\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}n}\mapsto\begin{cases}{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}0}&\textsf{if }{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}n=0}\\
\llbracket{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}e}\rrbracket({\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}n-1})&\textsf{otherwise}\end{cases}

\displaystyle\textsf{By compiling}

\displaystyle={\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}n}\mapsto\begin{cases}{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}0}&\textsf{if }{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}n=0}\\
\llbracket{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}v}\rrbracket({\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}n-1})&\textsf{otherwise}\end{cases}

\displaystyle\textsf{By induction}

\displaystyle=\llbracket{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\textsf{succ}(v)}\rrbracket

\displaystyle\textsf{By compiling}

\triangleright

Case

{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}e_{1}e_{2}}\Downarrow{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}v}

By assumption
{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}e_{1}}\Downarrow{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\lambda x.e}
and
{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}e_{2}}\Downarrow{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}v_{2}}
and
{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\{x\mapsto v_{2}\}(e)}\Downarrow{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}v}

By assumption
{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}e_{1}e_{2}}:{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\tau_{2}}

By inversion on typing
{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}e_{1}}:{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\tau_{1}\multimap\tau_{2}}
and
{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}e_{2}}:{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\tau_{1}}

Because evaluation preserves typing
{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\lambda x.e}:{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\tau_{1}\multimap\tau_{2}}
and
{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}v_{2}}:{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\tau_{1}}

By inversion on typing
{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}x\!:\!\tau_{1}}\vdash{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}e}:{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\tau_{2}}

Because closing substitutions preserve typing
{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\{x\mapsto v_{2}\}(e)}:{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\tau_{2}}

\displaystyle\llbracket{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}e_{1}e_{2}}\rrbracket

\displaystyle=\llbracket{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}e_{1}}\rrbracket(\llbracket{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}e_{2}}\rrbracket)

\displaystyle\textsf{By compiling}

\displaystyle=\llbracket{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\lambda x.e}\rrbracket(\llbracket{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}v_{2}}\rrbracket)

\displaystyle\textsf{By induction}

\displaystyle=({\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\vec{x}}\mapsto\llbracket{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}e}\rrbracket{({\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\vec{x}})})(\llbracket{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}v_{2}}\rrbracket)

\displaystyle\textsf{By compiling}

\displaystyle=\llbracket{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}e}\rrbracket({\llbracket{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}v_{2}}\rrbracket})

\displaystyle\textsf{By function application}

\displaystyle=\llbracket{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\{x\mapsto v_{2}\}(e)}\rrbracket

\displaystyle\textsf{Compiling commutes with substitution}

\displaystyle=\llbracket{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}v}\rrbracket

\displaystyle\textsf{By induction}

\triangleright

&=\llbracket{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}v_{1}}\rrbracket&&\textsf{By addition}\end{aligned}

\begin{aligned} &\,\,\;\;\;\,\llbracket{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\textsf{iter}\{{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}e_{1}}\mid{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}y}\hookrightarrow{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}e_{2}}\}({\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}e_{3}})}\rrbracket\\[5.0pt]

&={\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\sum_{n\in\mathbb{N}}}\,\llbracket{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}e_{3}}\rrbracket({\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}n})\,{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\cdot}\,\llbracket{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}e_{2}}\rrbracket(\llbracket{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}e_{2}}\rrbracket^{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}(n-1)}(\llbracket{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}e_{1}}\rrbracket))&&\textsf{Because }{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}f}^{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}n}({\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}v})={\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}f}({\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}f}^{{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}(n-1)}}({\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}v}))\\

&=\llbracket{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\textsf{succ}(v_{3})}}\rrbracket({\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}0})\,{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\cdot}\,\llbracket{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}e_{2}}\rrbracket^{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}0}(\llbracket{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}e_{1}}\rrbracket)\hskip 0.50003pt\,\,{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}+}\,\,\hskip 0.50003pt{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\sum_{n\in\mathbb{N}\setminus 0}}\,\llbracket{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}{{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\textsf{succ}(v_{3})}}}\rrbracket({\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}n})\,{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\cdot}\,\llbracket{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}e_{2}}\rrbracket(\llbracket{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}e_{2}}\rrbracket^{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}(n-1)}(\llbracket{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}e_{1}}\rrbracket))&&\textsf{Because }{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\sum_{n\in\mathbb{N}}}\,{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}f}({\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}n})={\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}f}({\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}0})\hskip 0.50003pt\,\,{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}+}\,\,\hskip 0.50003pt{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\sum_{n\in\mathbb{N}\setminus 0}}\,{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}f}({\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}n})\\
&={\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}0}\,{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\cdot}\,\llbracket{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}e_{2}}\rrbracket^{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}0}(\llbracket{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}e_{1}}\rrbracket)\hskip 0.50003pt\,\,{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}+}\,\,\hskip 0.50003pt{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\sum_{n\in\mathbb{N}\setminus 0}}\,\llbracket{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}{v_{3}}}\rrbracket({\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}n-1})\,{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\cdot}\,\llbracket{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}e_{2}}\rrbracket(\llbracket{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}e_{2}}\rrbracket^{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}(n-1)}(\llbracket{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}e_{1}}\rrbracket))&&\textsf{By compiling}\\
&={\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\sum_{n\in\mathbb{N}\setminus 0}}\,\llbracket{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}{v_{3}}}\rrbracket({\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}n-1})\,{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\cdot}\,\llbracket{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}e_{2}}\rrbracket(\llbracket{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}e_{2}}\rrbracket^{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}(n-1)}(\llbracket{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}e_{1}}\rrbracket))&&\textsf{By scalar multiplication}\\
&={\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\sum_{n\in\mathbb{N}\setminus 0}}\,\llbracket{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}e_{2}}\rrbracket(\llbracket{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}{v_{3}}}\rrbracket({\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}n-1})\,{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\cdot}\,\llbracket{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}e_{2}}\rrbracket^{{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}(n-1)}}(\llbracket{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}e_{1}}\rrbracket))&&\textsf{Compiling maps to multilinear maps}\\
&=\llbracket{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}e_{2}}\rrbracket({\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\sum_{n\in\mathbb{N}\setminus 0}}\,\llbracket{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}{v_{3}}}\rrbracket({\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}n-1})\,{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\cdot}\,\llbracket{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}e_{2}}\rrbracket^{{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}(n-1)}}(\llbracket{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}e_{1}}\rrbracket))&&\textsf{Compiling maps to multilinear maps}\\
&=\llbracket{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}e_{2}}\rrbracket\left(\,{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\sum_{n\in\mathbb{N}}}\,\llbracket{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}{v_{3}}}\rrbracket({\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}n})\,{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\cdot}\,\llbracket{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}e_{2}}\rrbracket^{{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}n}}(\llbracket{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}e_{1}}\rrbracket)\right)&&\textsf{Because }{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\sum_{n\in\mathbb{N}\setminus 0}{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}f}}({\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}n-1})={\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\sum_{n\in\mathbb{N}}{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}f}}({\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}n})\\[5.0pt]
&=\llbracket{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}e_{2}}\rrbracket(\llbracket{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\textsf{iter}\{{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}e_{1}}\mid{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}y}\hookrightarrow{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}e_{2}}\}({\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}v_{3}})}\rrbracket)&&\textsf{By compiling}\\
&=\llbracket{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}e_{2}}\rrbracket(\llbracket{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}v_{\textsf{n}}}\rrbracket)&&\textsf{By induction}\\
&=\llbracket{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\{y\mapsto v_{\textsf{n}}\}(e_{2})}\rrbracket&&\textsf{Compiling commutes with substitution}\\
&=\llbracket{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}v}\rrbracket&&\textsf{By induction}\end{aligned}

∎

B.6.
Lemma (Compiler preserves what programs won’t do)

{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\varnothing}\vdash{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}e}:{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\textsf{base}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}(}\tau{\color[rgb]{0,0,0})}}\,\textsf{ and }\,{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}e}\;\cancel{\Downarrow}\;{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}v}\implies\llbracket{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}e}\rrbracket\neq\llbracket{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}v}\rrbracket

Closed programs produce values, thus
{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}e}\Downarrow{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}v_{1}}
where
{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}v_{1}}\neq{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}v}
. Now consider the boolean case. Because of canonical forms
{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}v}
and
{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}v_{1}}
are either
tt
or
ff
, but cannot be equal. Without loss of generality, suppose
{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}v}={\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\textsf{ff}}}
and
{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}v_{1}}={\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\textsf{tt}}}
. By negation
\llbracket{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}e}\rrbracket=\llbracket{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}v}\rrbracket
, but the compiler preserves what programs do. Therefore we have a contradiction.

\llbracket{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}e}\rrbracket=\llbracket{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\textsf{tt}}}\rrbracket={\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\begin{bmatrix}1&\!\!\!0\end{bmatrix}}=\llbracket{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\textsf{ff}}}\rrbracket={\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\begin{bmatrix}0&\!\!\!1\end{bmatrix}}\vskip 3.00003pt

A similar argument holds for the natural numbers case.

∎

B.7.
Lemma (Compiler maps programs to multilinear maps)

{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\Delta,x\!:\!\tau_{1}}\vdash{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}e}:{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\tau_{2}}\implies\llbracket{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}e}\rrbracket({\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\vec{\sigma}},{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\alpha_{1}}\hskip 0.50003pt\,{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\cdot}\,\hskip 0.50003pt{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\vec{x_{1}}}\hskip 0.50003pt\,\,{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}+}\,\,\hskip 0.50003pt{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\alpha_{2}}\hskip 0.50003pt\,{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\cdot}\,\hskip 0.50003pt{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\vec{x_{2}}})={\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\alpha_{1}}\hskip 0.50003pt\,{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\cdot}\,\hskip 0.50003pt\llbracket{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}e}\rrbracket({\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\vec{\sigma}},{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\vec{x_{1}}})\hskip 0.50003pt\,\,{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}+}\,\,\hskip 0.50003pt{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\alpha_{2}}\hskip 0.50003pt\,{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\cdot}\,\hskip 0.50003pt\llbracket{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}e}\rrbracket({\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\vec{\sigma}},{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\vec{x_{2}}})

Immediate from compiler mapping programs to additive and homogeneous maps.

∎

B.8.
Lemma (Compiler maps programs to additive maps)

{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\Delta,x\!:\!\tau_{1}}\vdash{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}e}:{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\tau_{2}}\implies\llbracket{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}e}\rrbracket({\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\vec{\sigma}},{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\vec{x_{1}}}\hskip 0.50003pt\,\,{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}+}\,\,\hskip 0.50003pt{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\vec{x_{2}}})=\llbracket{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}e}\rrbracket({\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\vec{\sigma}},{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\vec{x_{1}}})\hskip 0.50003pt\,\,{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}+}\,\,\hskip 0.50003pt\llbracket{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}e}\rrbracket({\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\vec{\sigma}},{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\vec{x_{2}}})

By induction on
{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}e}
,

\triangleright

Case

{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}y}

Consider whether
{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}x}={\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}y}
,

\triangleright

Case

{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}x}={\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}y}

By inversion
{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\Delta}={\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\varnothing}

\displaystyle\llbracket{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}x}\rrbracket({\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\vec{x}}\hskip 0.50003pt\,\,{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}+}\,\,\hskip 0.50003pt{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\vec{y}})

\displaystyle={\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\vec{x}}\hskip 0.50003pt\,\,{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}+}\,\,\hskip 0.50003pt{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\vec{y}}

\displaystyle\textsf{By compiling}

\displaystyle=\llbracket{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}x}\rrbracket({\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\vec{x}})\hskip 0.50003pt\,\,{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}+}\,\,\hskip 0.50003pt\llbracket{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}y}\rrbracket({\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\vec{y}})

\displaystyle\textsf{By compiling}

\triangleright

Case

{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}x}\neq{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}y}

Vacuous because we assume a contradiction
{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\Delta,x\!:\!\tau_{1}}\vdash{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}y}:{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\tau_{2}}

\triangleright

Case

tt

Vacuous because we assume
{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\Delta,x\!:\!\tau_{1}}\vdash{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\textsf{tt}}}:{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\tau_{2}}

\triangleright

Case

ff

Vacuous because we assume
{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\Delta,x\!:\!\tau_{1}}\vdash{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\textsf{ff}}}:{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\tau_{2}}

\triangleright

Case

0

Vacuous because we assume
{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\Delta,x\!:\!\tau_{1}}\vdash{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\textsf{0}}}:{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\tau_{2}}

\triangleright

Case

{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\textsf{succ}(e)}

By inversion
{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\Delta,x\!:\!\tau}\vdash{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}e}:{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\mathbb{N}}

\begin{aligned} &\,\,\,\,\,\,\,\,\llbracket{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\textsf{succ}(e)}}\rrbracket({\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\vec{\sigma_{1}}}\hskip 0.50003pt\,\,{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}+}\,\,\hskip 0.50003pt{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\vec{\sigma_{2}}})&&\\
&={\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}n}\mapsto\begin{cases}{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}0}&\textsf{if }{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}n=0}\\
\llbracket{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}e}\rrbracket({\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\vec{\sigma}},{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\vec{x_{1}}}\hskip 0.50003pt\,\,{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}+}\,\,\hskip 0.50003pt{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\vec{\sigma_{2}}})({\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}n-1})&\textsf{otherwise}\end{cases}&&\textsf{By compiling}\\
&={\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}n}\mapsto\begin{cases}{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}0}&\textsf{if }{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}n=0}\\
\llbracket{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}e}\rrbracket({\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\vec{\sigma}},{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\vec{x_{1}}})({\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}n-1})\hskip 0.50003pt\,\,{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}+}\,\,\hskip 0.50003pt\llbracket{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}e}\rrbracket({\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\vec{\sigma}},{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\vec{x_{2}}})({\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}n-1})&\textsf{otherwise}\end{cases}&&\textsf{By induction}\\
&=\Biggl({\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}n}\mapsto\begin{cases}{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}0}&\textsf{if }{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}n=0}\\
\llbracket{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}e}\rrbracket({\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\vec{\sigma_{1}}})({\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}n-1})&\textsf{otherwise}\end{cases}\;\Biggr)\hskip 0.50003pt\,\,{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}+}\,\,\hskip 0.50003pt\Biggl({\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}n}\mapsto\begin{cases}{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}0}&\textsf{if }{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}n=0}\\
\llbracket{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}e}\rrbracket({\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\vec{\sigma_{2}}})({\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}n-1})&\textsf{otherwise}\end{cases}\;\Biggr)&&\textsf{By addition of functions}\\
&=\llbracket{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\textsf{succ}(e)}}\rrbracket({\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\vec{\sigma_{1}}})\hskip 0.50003pt\,\,{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}+}\,\,\hskip 0.50003pt\llbracket{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\textsf{succ}(e)}}\rrbracket({\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\vec{\sigma_{2}}})&&\textsf{By compiling}\end{aligned}

\triangleright

Case

{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\lambda y.e}

Consider whether
{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}x}={\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}y}
,

\triangleright

Case

{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}x}={\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}y}

Vacuous by Barendregt’s convention: free and bound variables must be unique.

\triangleright

Case

{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}x}\neq{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}y}

By inversion on typing
{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\Delta,x\!:\!\tau,y\!:\!\tau_{1}}\vdash{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}e}:{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\tau_{2}}

\displaystyle\llbracket{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\lambda y.e}\rrbracket({\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\vec{\sigma}},{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\vec{x_{1}}}\hskip 0.50003pt\,\,{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}+}\,\,\hskip 0.50003pt{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\vec{x_{2}}})

\displaystyle={\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\vec{y}}\mapsto\llbracket{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}e}\rrbracket({\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\vec{\sigma}},{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\vec{x_{1}}}\hskip 0.50003pt\,\,{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}+}\,\,\hskip 0.50003pt{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\vec{x_{2}}},{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\vec{y}})

\displaystyle\textsf{By compiling}

\displaystyle={\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\vec{y}}\mapsto\llbracket{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}e}\rrbracket({\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\vec{\sigma}},{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\vec{x_{1}}}\hskip 0.50003pt\,\,{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}+}\,\,\hskip 0.50003pt{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\vec{x_{2}}},{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\vec{y}})_{{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\Delta,y:\tau_{1},x:\tau}}

\displaystyle\textsf{Compiling invariant to exchange}

\displaystyle={\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\vec{y}}\mapsto\llbracket{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}e}\rrbracket({\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\vec{\sigma}},{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\vec{y}},{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\vec{x_{1}}}\hskip 0.50003pt\,\,{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}+}\,\,\hskip 0.50003pt{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\vec{x_{2}}})

\displaystyle\textsf{By restricting environments}

\displaystyle={\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\vec{y}}\mapsto\llbracket{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}e}\rrbracket({\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\vec{\sigma}},{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\vec{y}},{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\vec{x_{1}}})\hskip 0.50003pt\,\,{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}+}\,\,\hskip 0.50003pt\llbracket{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}e}\rrbracket({\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\vec{\sigma}},{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\vec{y}},{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\vec{x_{2}}})

\displaystyle\textsf{By induction}

\displaystyle={\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\vec{y}}\mapsto\llbracket{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}e}\rrbracket({\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\vec{\sigma}},{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\vec{y}},{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\vec{x_{1}}})_{{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\Delta,x:\tau,y:\tau_{1}}}\hskip 0.50003pt\,\,{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}+}\,\,\hskip 0.50003pt\llbracket{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}e}\rrbracket({\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\vec{\sigma}},{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\vec{y}},{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\vec{x_{2}}})_{{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\Delta,x:\tau,y:\tau_{1}}}

\displaystyle\textsf{Compiling invariant to exchange}

\displaystyle={\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\vec{y}}\mapsto\llbracket{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}e}\rrbracket({\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\vec{\sigma}},{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\vec{x_{1}}},{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\vec{y}})\hskip 0.50003pt\,\,{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}+}\,\,\hskip 0.50003pt\llbracket{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}e}\rrbracket({\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\vec{\sigma}},{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\vec{x_{2}}},{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\vec{y}})

\displaystyle\textsf{By restricting environments}

\displaystyle=\bigl({\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\vec{y}}\mapsto\llbracket{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}e}\rrbracket({\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\vec{\sigma}},{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\vec{x_{1}}},{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\vec{y}})\bigr)\hskip 0.50003pt\,\,{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}+}\,\,\hskip 0.50003pt\bigl({\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\vec{y}}\mapsto\llbracket{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}e}\rrbracket({\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\vec{\sigma}},{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\vec{x_{2}}},{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\vec{y}})\bigr)

\displaystyle\textsf{By addition of functions}

\displaystyle=\llbracket{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\lambda y.e}\rrbracket({\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\vec{\sigma}},{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\vec{x_{1}}})\hskip 0.50003pt\,\,{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}+}\,\,\hskip 0.50003pt\llbracket{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\lambda y.e}\rrbracket({\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\vec{\sigma}},{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\vec{x_{2}}})

\displaystyle\textsf{By compiling}

\triangleright

Case

{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\textsf{if}\;e_{1}\;\textsf{then}\;e_{2}\;\textsf{else}\;e_{3}}

By inversion on typing
{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\Delta,x\!:\!\tau_{1}}={\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\Delta_{1}\circ\Delta_{2}}
and
{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\Delta_{1}}\vdash{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}e_{1}}:{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\mathbb{2}}
and
{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\Delta_{2}}\vdash{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}e_{2}}:{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\tau}
and
{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\Delta_{2}}\vdash{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}e_{3}}:{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\tau}

Consider whether
{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}x}\in{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\textsf{fv}}({\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}e_{1}}})
or
({\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}x}\in{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\textsf{fv}}({\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}e_{2}}})\land{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}x}\in{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\textsf{fv}}({\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}e_{3}}}))
,

\triangleright

Case

{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}x}\in{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\textsf{fv}}({\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}e_{1}})

Because contexts permit exchange
{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\Delta_{11}},x\!:\!\tau_{1}}\vdash{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}e_{1}}:{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\mathbb{2}}

\begin{aligned} &\,\,\,\,\,\,\,\,\llbracket{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\textsf{if}\;e_{1}\;\textsf{then}\;e_{2}\;\textsf{else}\;e_{3}}\rrbracket({\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\vec{\sigma}},{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\vec{x_{1}}}\hskip 0.50003pt\,\,{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}+}\,\,\hskip 0.50003pt{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\vec{x_{2}}})\\
&={\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\pi_{1}}(\llbracket{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}e_{1}}\rrbracket({\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\vec{\sigma}},{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\vec{x_{1}}}\hskip 0.50003pt\,\,{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}+}\,\,\hskip 0.50003pt{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\vec{x_{2}}})_{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\Delta_{1}})\hskip 0.50003pt\,{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\cdot}\,\hskip 0.50003pt\llbracket{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}e_{2}}\rrbracket({\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\vec{\sigma}},{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\vec{x_{1}}}\hskip 0.50003pt\,\,{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}+}\,\,\hskip 0.50003pt{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\vec{x_{2}}})_{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\Delta_{2}}\\
&\hskip 0.50003pt\,\,{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}+}\,\,\hskip 0.50003pt{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\pi_{2}}(\llbracket{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}e_{1}}\rrbracket({\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\vec{\sigma}},{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\vec{x_{1}}}\hskip 0.50003pt\,\,{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}+}\,\,\hskip 0.50003pt{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\vec{x_{2}}})_{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\Delta_{1}})\hskip 0.50003pt\,{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\cdot}\,\hskip 0.50003pt\llbracket{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}e_{3}}\rrbracket({\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\vec{\sigma}},{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\vec{x_{1}}}\hskip 0.50003pt\,\,{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}+}\,\,\hskip 0.50003pt{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\vec{x_{2}}})_{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\Delta_{2}}&&\textsf{By compiling}\\
&={\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\pi_{1}}(\llbracket{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}e_{1}}\rrbracket(({\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\vec{\sigma}},{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\vec{x_{1}}}\hskip 0.50003pt\,\,{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}+}\,\,\hskip 0.50003pt{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\vec{x_{2}}})_{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\Delta_{1}})_{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\Delta_{11}},x:\tau_{1}})\hskip 0.50003pt\,{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\cdot}\,\hskip 0.50003pt\llbracket{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}e_{2}}\rrbracket({\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\vec{\sigma}},{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\vec{x_{1}}}\hskip 0.50003pt\,\,{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}+}\,\,\hskip 0.50003pt{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\vec{x_{2}}})_{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\Delta_{2}}\\
&\hskip 0.50003pt\,\,{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}+}\,\,\hskip 0.50003pt{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\pi_{2}}(\llbracket{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}e_{1}}\rrbracket(({\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\vec{\sigma}},{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\vec{x_{1}}}\hskip 0.50003pt\,\,{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}+}\,\,\hskip 0.50003pt{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\vec{x_{2}}})_{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\Delta_{1}})_{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\Delta_{11}},x:\tau_{1}})\hskip 0.50003pt\,{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\cdot}\,\hskip 0.50003pt\llbracket{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}e_{3}}\rrbracket({\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\vec{\sigma}},{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\vec{x_{1}}}\hskip 0.50003pt\,\,{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}+}\,\,\hskip 0.50003pt{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\vec{x_{2}}})_{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\Delta_{2}}&&\textsf{Compiling invariant to exchange}\\
&={\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\pi_{1}}(\llbracket{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}e_{1}}\rrbracket({\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\vec{\sigma}},{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\vec{x_{1}}}\hskip 0.50003pt\,\,{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}+}\,\,\hskip 0.50003pt{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\vec{x_{2}}})_{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\Delta_{11}},x:\tau_{1}})\hskip 0.50003pt\,{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\cdot}\,\hskip 0.50003pt\llbracket{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}e_{2}}\rrbracket({\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\vec{\sigma}},{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\vec{x_{1}}}\hskip 0.50003pt\,\,{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}+}\,\,\hskip 0.50003pt{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\vec{x_{2}}})_{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\Delta_{2}}\\

&\hskip 0.50003pt\,\,{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}+}\,\,\hskip 0.50003pt{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\sum_{n\in\mathbb{N}}}\,\llbracket{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}e_{3}}}\rrbracket{({\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\vec{\sigma}},{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\vec{x_{1}}}\hskip 0.50003pt\,\,{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}+}\,\,\hskip 0.50003pt{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\vec{x_{2}}})_{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\Delta_{3}}}({\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}n})\,{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\cdot}\,(\llbracket{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}e_{2}}}\rrbracket{({\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\vec{\sigma}},{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\vec{x_{1}}}\hskip 0.50003pt\,\,{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}+}\,\,\hskip 0.50003pt{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\vec{x_{2}}})_{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\Delta_{2}})}^{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}n}(\llbracket{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}e_{1}}}\rrbracket{({\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\vec{\sigma}},{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\vec{x_{2}}})_{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\Delta_{1}})}&&\textsf{Because }{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\sum_{n\in\mathbb{N}}}\,{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}f}({\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}n})\hskip 0.50003pt\,\,{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}+}\,\,\hskip 0.50003pt{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}g}({\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}n})={\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\sum_{n\in\mathbb{N}}}\,{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}f}({\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}n})\hskip 0.50003pt\,\,{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}+}\,\,\hskip 0.50003pt{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\sum_{n\in\mathbb{N}}}\,{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}g}({\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}n})\\
&={\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\sum_{n\in\mathbb{N}}}\,\llbracket{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}e_{3}}}\rrbracket{({\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\vec{\sigma}},{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\vec{x_{1}}})_{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\Delta_{3}}}({\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}n})\,{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\cdot}\,(\llbracket{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}e_{2}}}\rrbracket{({\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\vec{\sigma}},{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\vec{x_{1}}}\hskip 0.50003pt\,\,{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}+}\,\,\hskip 0.50003pt{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\vec{x_{2}}})_{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\Delta_{2}})}^{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}n}(\llbracket{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}e_{1}}}\rrbracket{({\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\vec{\sigma}},{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\vec{x_{1}}})_{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\Delta_{1}})}\\
&\hskip 0.50003pt\,\,{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}+}\,\,\hskip 0.50003pt{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\sum_{n\in\mathbb{N}}}\,\llbracket{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}e_{3}}}\rrbracket{({\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\vec{\sigma}},{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\vec{x_{2}}})_{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\Delta_{3}}}({\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}n})\,{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\cdot}\,(\llbracket{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}e_{2}}}\rrbracket{({\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\vec{\sigma}},{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\vec{x_{1}}}\hskip 0.50003pt\,\,{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}+}\,\,\hskip 0.50003pt{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\vec{x_{2}}})_{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\Delta_{2}})}^{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}n}(\llbracket{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}e_{1}}}\rrbracket{({\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\vec{\sigma}},{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\vec{x_{2}}})_{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\Delta_{1}})}&&\textsf{Because }({\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\vec{\sigma}},{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\vec{x_{1}}})_{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\Delta_{3}}=({\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\vec{\sigma}},{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\vec{x_{2}}})_{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\Delta_{3}}=({\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\vec{\sigma}},{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\vec{x_{1}}}\hskip 0.50003pt\,\,{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}+}\,\,\hskip 0.50003pt{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\vec{x_{2}}})_{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\Delta_{3}}\\
&={\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\sum_{n\in\mathbb{N}}}\,\llbracket{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}e_{3}}}\rrbracket{({\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\vec{\sigma}},{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\vec{x_{1}}})_{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\Delta_{3}}}({\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}n})\,{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\cdot}\,(\llbracket{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}e_{2}}}\rrbracket{({\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\vec{\sigma}},{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\vec{x_{1}}})_{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\Delta_{2}})}^{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}n}(\llbracket{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}e_{1}}}\rrbracket{({\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\vec{\sigma}},{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\vec{x_{1}}})_{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\Delta_{1}})}\\
&\hskip 0.50003pt\,\,{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}+}\,\,\hskip 0.50003pt{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\sum_{n\in\mathbb{N}}}\,\llbracket{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}e_{3}}}\rrbracket{({\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\vec{\sigma}},{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\vec{x_{2}}})_{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\Delta_{3}}}({\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}n})\,{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\cdot}\,(\llbracket{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}e_{2}}}\rrbracket{({\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\vec{\sigma}},{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\vec{x_{2}}})_{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\Delta_{2}})}^{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}n}(\llbracket{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}e_{1}}}\rrbracket{({\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\vec{\sigma}},{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\vec{x_{2}}})_{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\Delta_{1}})}&&\textsf{Because }({\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\vec{\sigma}},{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\vec{x_{1}}})_{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\Delta_{2}}=({\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\vec{\sigma}},{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\vec{x_{2}}})_{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\Delta_{2}}=({\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\vec{\sigma}},{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\vec{x_{1}}}\hskip 0.50003pt\,\,{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}+}\,\,\hskip 0.50003pt{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\vec{x_{2}}})_{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\Delta_{2}}\\
&=\llbracket{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\textsf{iter}\{{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}e_{1}}\mid{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}y}\hookrightarrow{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}e_{2}}\}({\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}e_{3}})}\rrbracket({\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\vec{\sigma}},{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\vec{x_{1}}})\hskip 0.50003pt\,\,{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}+}\,\,\hskip 0.50003pt\llbracket{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\textsf{iter}\{{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}e_{1}}\mid{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}y}\hookrightarrow{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}e_{2}}\}({\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}e_{3}})}\rrbracket({\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\vec{\sigma}},{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\vec{x_{2}}})&&\textsf{By compiling}\end{aligned}

\triangleright

Case

{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}x}\in{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\textsf{fv}}({\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}e_{2}})

Similar to previous case

\triangleright

Case

{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}x}\in{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\textsf{fv}}({\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}e_{3}})

Similar to previous case

∎

B.9.
Lemma (Compiler maps programs to homogeneous maps)

{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\Delta,x\!:\!\tau_{1}}\vdash{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}e}:{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\tau_{2}}\implies{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\alpha}\hskip 0.50003pt\,{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\cdot}\,\hskip 0.50003pt\llbracket{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}e}\rrbracket({\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\vec{\sigma}},{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\vec{x}})

By induction on
{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}e}
, similar to showing the compiler maps programs to additive maps.

∎

B.10.
Lemma (Compiler commutes with substitution)

{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\Delta,x\!:\!\tau_{1}}\vdash{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}e}:{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\tau_{2}}\implies\llbracket{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\{x\mapsto v\}(e)}\rrbracket({\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\vec{\sigma}})=\llbracket{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}e}\rrbracket({\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\vec{\sigma}},\llbracket{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}v}\rrbracket)

By induction on
{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}e}
,

\triangleright

Case

{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}y}

Consider whether
{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}x}={\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}y}

\triangleright

Case

{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}x}={\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}y}

By inversion on typing
\Delta={\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\varnothing}

\displaystyle\llbracket{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\{x\mapsto v\}(x)}\rrbracket

\displaystyle=\llbracket{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}v}\rrbracket

\displaystyle\textsf{By substitution}

\displaystyle=\llbracket{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}x}\rrbracket(\llbracket{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}v}\rrbracket)

\displaystyle\textsf{By compiling}

\triangleright

Case

{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}x}\neq{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}y}

Vacuous because we assume
{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\Delta,x\!:\!\tau_{1}}\vdash{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}y}:{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\tau_{2}}

\triangleright

Case

tt

Vacuous because we assume
{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\Delta,x\!:\!\tau_{1}}\vdash{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\textsf{tt}}}:{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\tau_{2}}

\triangleright

Case

ff

Vacuous because we assume
{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\Delta,x\!:\!\tau_{1}}\vdash{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\textsf{ff}}}:{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\tau_{2}}

\triangleright

Case

0

Vacuous because we assume
{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\Delta,x\!:\!\tau_{1}}\vdash{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\textsf{0}}}:{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\tau_{2}}

\triangleright

Case

{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\textsf{succ}(e)}

By inversion on typing
{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\Delta,x\!:\!\tau}\vdash{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}e}:{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\mathbb{N}}

\displaystyle\llbracket{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\{x\mapsto v\}({\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\textsf{succ}(e)})}\rrbracket({\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\vec{\sigma}})

\displaystyle=\llbracket{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\textsf{succ}(\{x\mapsto v\}(e))}}\rrbracket({\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\vec{\sigma}})

\displaystyle\textsf{By substitution}

\displaystyle={\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}n}\mapsto\begin{cases}{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}0}&\textsf{if }{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}n=0}\\
\llbracket{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\{x\mapsto v\}(e)}\rrbracket({\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\vec{\sigma}})({\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}n-1})&\textsf{otherwise}\end{cases}

\displaystyle\textsf{By compiling}

\displaystyle={\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}n}\mapsto\begin{cases}{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}0}&\textsf{if }{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}n=0}\\
\llbracket{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}e}\rrbracket({\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\vec{\sigma}},\llbracket{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}v}\rrbracket)({\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}n-1})&\textsf{otherwise}\end{cases}

\displaystyle\textsf{By induction}

\displaystyle=\llbracket{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\textsf{succ}(e)}}\rrbracket({\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\vec{\sigma}},\llbracket{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}v}\rrbracket)

\displaystyle\textsf{By compiling}

\triangleright

Case

{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\lambda y.e}

Consider whether
{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}x}\neq{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}y}
,

\triangleright

Case

{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}x}\neq{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}y}

By inversion on typing
{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\Delta,x\!:\!\tau,y\!:\!\tau_{1}}\vdash{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}e}:{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\tau_{2}}

Because contexts permit exchange
{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\Delta,y\!:\!\tau_{1},x\!:\!\tau}\vdash{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}e}:{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\tau_{2}}

Because substitution preserves typing
{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\Delta,y\!:\!\tau_{1}}\vdash{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\{x\mapsto v\}(e)}:{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\tau_{2}}

\displaystyle\llbracket{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\{x\mapsto v\}(\lambda y.e)}\rrbracket({\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\vec{\sigma}})

\displaystyle=\llbracket{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\lambda y.\{x\mapsto v\}(e)}\rrbracket({\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\vec{\sigma}})

\displaystyle\textsf{By substitution}

\displaystyle={\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\vec{y}}\mapsto\llbracket{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\{x\mapsto v\}(e)}\rrbracket({\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\vec{\sigma}},{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\vec{y}})

\displaystyle\textsf{By compiling}

\displaystyle={\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\vec{y}}\mapsto\llbracket{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}e}\rrbracket({\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\vec{\sigma}},{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\vec{y}},\llbracket{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}v}\rrbracket)

\displaystyle\textsf{By induction}

\displaystyle={\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\vec{y}}\mapsto\llbracket{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}e}\rrbracket({\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\vec{\sigma}},{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\vec{y}},\llbracket{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}v}\rrbracket)_{{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\Delta,x:\tau,y:\tau_{1}}}

\displaystyle\textsf{Compiling is exchange invariant }

\displaystyle={\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\vec{y}}\mapsto\llbracket{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}e}\rrbracket({\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\vec{\sigma}},\llbracket{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}v}\rrbracket,{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\vec{y}})

\displaystyle\textsf{By restricting environments}

\displaystyle=\llbracket{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\lambda y.e}\rrbracket({\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\vec{\sigma}},\llbracket{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}v}\rrbracket)

\displaystyle\textsf{By compiling}

\triangleright

Case

{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}x}={\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}y}

Vacuous by Barendregt’s convention: free and bound variables must be distinct.

\triangleright

Case

{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}e_{1}e_{2}}

By typing
{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}x}\in{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\textsf{fv}}({\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}e_{1}e_{2}})

Consider whether
{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}x}\in{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\textsf{fv}}({\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}e_{1}})
or
{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}x}\in{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\textsf{fv}}({\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}e_{2}})
,

\triangleright

Case

{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}x}\in{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\textsf{fv}}({\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}e_{1}})

By assumption
({\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\vec{\sigma}},\llbracket{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}v}\rrbracket)\in\llbracket{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\Delta,x\!:\!\tau}\rrbracket

By inversion
{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\Delta}={\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\Delta_{11}\circ\Delta_{12}}
and
{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\Delta_{11}}\vdash{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\{x\mapsto v\}(e_{1})}:{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\tau_{1}\multimap\tau_{2}}
and
{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\Delta_{12}}\vdash{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}e_{2}}:{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\tau_{1}}

By inversion
{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\Delta,x\!:\!\tau}={\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\Delta_{21}\circ\Delta_{22}}
and
{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\Delta_{21}}\vdash{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}e_{1}}:{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\tau_{1}\multimap\tau_{2}}
and

&={\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\sum_{n\in\mathbb{N}}}\,\llbracket{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}e_{3}}}\rrbracket{({\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\vec{\sigma}}_{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\Delta_{13}})_{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\Delta_{23}}}({\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}n})\,{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\cdot}\,\llbracket{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}e_{2}}}\rrbracket{(({\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\vec{\sigma}}_{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\Delta_{12}})_{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\Delta_{22}})}^{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}n}(\llbracket{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}e_{1}}}\rrbracket(({\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\vec{\sigma}},\llbracket{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}v}\rrbracket)_{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\Delta_{21}})&&\textsf{Because }({\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\vec{\sigma}}_{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\Delta_{11}},\llbracket{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}v}\rrbracket)_{{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\Delta_{21}}}=({\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\vec{\sigma}},\llbracket{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}v}\rrbracket)_{{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\Delta_{21}}}\\
&={\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\sum_{n\in\mathbb{N}}}\,\llbracket{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}e_{3}}}\rrbracket{({\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\vec{\sigma}}_{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\Delta_{13}})_{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\Delta_{23}}}({\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}n})\,{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\cdot}\,\llbracket{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}e_{2}}}\rrbracket{(({\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\vec{\sigma}},\llbracket{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}v}\rrbracket)_{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\Delta_{22}})}^{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}n}(\llbracket{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}e_{1}}}\rrbracket(({\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\vec{\sigma}},\llbracket{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}v}\rrbracket)_{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\Delta_{21}})&&\textsf{Because }({\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\vec{\sigma}}_{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\Delta_{12}})_{{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\Delta_{22}}}=({\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\vec{\sigma}},\llbracket{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}v}\rrbracket)_{{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\Delta_{22}}}\\
&={\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\sum_{n\in\mathbb{N}}}\,\llbracket{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}e_{3}}}\rrbracket{({\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\vec{\sigma}},\llbracket{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}v}\rrbracket)_{{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\Delta_{23}}}}({\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}n})\,{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\cdot}\,\llbracket{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}e_{2}}}\rrbracket{(({\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\vec{\sigma}},\llbracket{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}v}\rrbracket)_{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\Delta_{22}})}^{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}n}(\llbracket{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}e_{1}}}\rrbracket(({\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\vec{\sigma}},\llbracket{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}v}\rrbracket)_{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\Delta_{21}})&&\textsf{Because }({\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\vec{\sigma}}_{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\Delta_{13}})_{{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\Delta_{23}}}=({\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\vec{\sigma}},\llbracket{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}v}\rrbracket)_{{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\Delta_{23}}}\\
&=\llbracket{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}e_{1}e_{2}}\rrbracket({\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\vec{\sigma}},\llbracket{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}v}\rrbracket)&&\textsf{By compiling}\end{aligned}

Case

\displaystyle=\llbracket{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\lambda x.e}\rrbracket({\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\vec{\sigma_{1}}})_{{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\Delta_{2}}}}

\displaystyle\textsf{By compiling}

\triangleright

Case

{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\Delta_{1}}\vdash{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}e_{1}e_{2}}:{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\tau_{2}}

By assumption
{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\Delta_{1}}={\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\Delta_{11}}\circ{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\Delta_{12}}}
and
{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\Delta_{11}}}\vdash{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}e_{1}}:{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\tau_{1}\multimap\tau_{2}}
and
{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\Delta_{12}}}\vdash{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}e_{2}}:{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\tau_{1}}

By assumption
{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\Delta_{2}}\vdash{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}e_{1}e_{2}}:{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\tau_{2}}
and
{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\Delta_{2}}={\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\Delta_{21}}\circ{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\Delta_{22}}}
and
{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\Delta_{21}}}\vdash{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}e_{1}}:{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\tau_{1}\multimap\tau_{2}}
and
{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\Delta_{22}}}\vdash{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}e_{2}}:{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\tau_{1}}

Because typing only admits exchange of contexts
{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\Delta_{1}}={\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\Delta_{11}}\circ{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\Delta_{12}}}={\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\Delta_{21}}\circ{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\Delta_{22}}}

\displaystyle\llbracket{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}e_{1}e_{2}}\rrbracket({\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\vec{\sigma_{1}}})

\displaystyle=\llbracket{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}e_{1}}\rrbracket({\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\vec{\sigma_{1}}})_{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\Delta_{11}}(\llbracket{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}e_{2}}\rrbracket({\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\vec{\sigma_{1}}})_{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\Delta_{12}})

\displaystyle\textsf{By compiling}

\displaystyle=\llbracket{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}e_{1}}\rrbracket(({\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\vec{\sigma_{1}}})_{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\Delta_{11}})_{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\Delta_{21}}(\llbracket{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}e_{2}}\rrbracket(({\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\vec{\sigma_{1}}})_{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\Delta_{12}})_{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\Delta_{22}})

\displaystyle\textsf{By induction}

\displaystyle=\llbracket{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}e_{1}}\rrbracket({\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\vec{\sigma_{1}}})_{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\Delta_{21}}(\llbracket{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}e_{2}}\rrbracket(({\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\vec{\sigma_{1}}})_{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\Delta_{12}})_{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\Delta_{22}})

\displaystyle\textsf{Because }(({\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\vec{\sigma_{1}}})_{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\Delta_{11}})_{{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\Delta_{21}}}=({\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\vec{\sigma_{1}}})_{{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\Delta_{21}}}

\displaystyle=\llbracket{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}e_{1}}\rrbracket({\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\vec{\sigma_{1}}})_{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\Delta_{21}}(\llbracket{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}e_{2}}\rrbracket({\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\vec{\sigma_{1}}})_{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\Delta_{22}})

\displaystyle\textsf{Because }(({\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\vec{\sigma_{1}}})_{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\Delta_{12}})_{{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\Delta_{22}}}=({\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\vec{\sigma_{1}}})_{{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\Delta_{22}}}

\displaystyle=\llbracket{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}e_{1}e_{2}}\rrbracket({\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\vec{\sigma_{1}}})_{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\Delta_{2}}

\displaystyle\textsf{By compiling}

\triangleright

Case

{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\Delta_{1}}\vdash{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\textsf{if}\;e_{1}\;\textsf{then}\;e_{2}\;\textsf{else}\;e_{3}}:{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\tau}

By inversion
{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\Delta}={\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\Delta_{11}\circ\Delta_{12}}
and
{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\Delta_{11}}\vdash{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\{x\mapsto v\}(e_{1})}:{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\mathbb{2}}

and

and
{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\Delta_{22}}\vdash{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}e_{3}}:{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\tau}

\displaystyle\llbracket{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\textsf{if}\;e_{1}\;\textsf{then}\;e_{2}\;\textsf{else}\;e_{3}}\rrbracket({\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\vec{\sigma}})

\displaystyle={\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\pi_{1}}(\llbracket{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}e_{1}}\rrbracket({\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\vec{\sigma}}_{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\Delta_{11}}))\hskip 0.50003pt\,{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\cdot}\,\hskip 0.50003pt\llbracket{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}e_{2}}\rrbracket({\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\vec{\sigma}})_{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\Delta_{12}}

\displaystyle\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}{\displaystyle+}\,\,\hskip 0.50003pt{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\pi_{2}}(\llbracket{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}e_{1}}\rrbracket({\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\vec{\sigma}}_{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\Delta_{11}}))\hskip 0.50003pt\,{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\cdot}\,\hskip 0.50003pt\llbracket{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}e_{3}}\rrbracket({\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\vec{\sigma}})_{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\Delta_{12}}

\displaystyle\textsf{By compiling}

\displaystyle={\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\pi_{1}}(\llbracket{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}e_{1}}\rrbracket({\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\vec{\sigma}}_{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\Delta_{11}})_{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\Delta_{21}})\hskip 0.50003pt\,{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\cdot}\,\hskip 0.50003pt\llbracket{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}e_{2}}\rrbracket(({\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\vec{\sigma}})_{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\Delta_{12}})_{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\Delta_{22}}

\displaystyle\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}{\displaystyle+}\,\,\hskip 0.50003pt{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\pi_{2}}(\llbracket{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}e_{1}}\rrbracket({\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\vec{\sigma}}_{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\Delta_{11}})_{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\Delta_{21}})\hskip 0.50003pt\,{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\cdot}\,\hskip 0.50003pt\llbracket{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}e_{3}}\rrbracket(({\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\vec{\sigma}})_{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\Delta_{12}})_{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\Delta_{22}}

\displaystyle\textsf{By induction}

\displaystyle={\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\pi_{1}}(\llbracket{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}e_{1}}\rrbracket({\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\vec{\sigma}}_{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\Delta_{21}}))\hskip 0.50003pt\,{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\cdot}\,\hskip 0.50003pt\llbracket{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}e_{2}}\rrbracket(({\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\vec{\sigma}})_{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\Delta_{12}})_{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\Delta_{22}}

\displaystyle\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}{\displaystyle+}\,\,\hskip 0.50003pt{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\pi_{2}}(\llbracket{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}e_{1}}\rrbracket({\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\vec{\sigma}}_{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\Delta_{21}}))\hskip 0.50003pt\,{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\cdot}\,\hskip 0.50003pt\llbracket{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}e_{3}}\rrbracket(({\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\vec{\sigma}})_{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\Delta_{12}})_{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\Delta_{22}}

\displaystyle\textsf{Because }({\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\vec{\sigma}}_{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\Delta_{11}})_{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\Delta_{21}}=({\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\vec{\sigma}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\Delta_{21}}})

\displaystyle={\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\pi_{1}}(\llbracket{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}e_{1}}\rrbracket({\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\vec{\sigma}}_{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\Delta_{21}}))\hskip 0.50003pt\,{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\cdot}\,\hskip 0.50003pt\llbracket{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}e_{2}}\rrbracket({\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\vec{\sigma}})_{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\Delta_{22}}

\displaystyle\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}{\displaystyle+}\,\,\hskip 0.50003pt{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\pi_{2}}(\llbracket{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}e_{1}}\rrbracket({\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\vec{\sigma}}_{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\Delta_{21}}))\hskip 0.50003pt\,{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\cdot}\,\hskip 0.50003pt\llbracket{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}e_{3}}\rrbracket({\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\vec{\sigma}})_{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\Delta_{22}}

\displaystyle\textsf{Because }(({\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\vec{\sigma}})_{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\Delta_{12}})_{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\Delta_{22}}=({\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\vec{\sigma}})_{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\Delta_{22}}

\displaystyle=\llbracket{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\textsf{if}\;e_{1}\;\textsf{then}\;e_{2}\;\textsf{else}\;e_{3}}\rrbracket({\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\vec{\sigma}})_{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\Delta_{2}}

\displaystyle\textsf{By compiling}

\triangleright

Case

{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\Delta_{1}\circ\Delta_{2}\circ\Delta_{3}}\vdash{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\textsf{iter}\{{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}e_{1}}\mid{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}y}\hookrightarrow{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}e_{2}}\}({\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}e_{3}})}:{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\tau}

By assumption
{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\Delta_{1}}={\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\Delta_{11}}\circ{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\Delta_{12}}\circ{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\Delta_{13}}}
and
{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\Delta_{11}}}\vdash{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}e_{1}}:{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\tau}
and
{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\Delta_{12}},y\!:\!\tau}\vdash{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}e_{2}}:{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\tau}
and
{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\Delta_{13}}}\vdash{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}e_{3}}:{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\mathbb{N}}

By assumption
{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\Delta_{2}}\vdash{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\textsf{iter}\{{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}e_{1}}\mid{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}y}\hookrightarrow{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}e_{2}}\}({\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}e_{3}})}:{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\tau_{2}}

By assumption
{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\Delta_{2}}={\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\Delta_{21}}\circ{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\Delta_{22}}\circ{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\Delta_{23}}}
and
{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\Delta_{21}}}\vdash{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}e_{1}}:{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\tau_{1}\multimap\tau_{2}}
and
{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\Delta_{22}}}\vdash{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}e_{2}}:{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\tau_{1}}
and
{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\Delta_{23}}}\vdash{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}e_{3}}:{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\tau}

Because typing only admits exchange of contexts
{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\Delta_{3}}={\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\Delta_{31}}\circ{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\Delta_{32}}\circ{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\Delta_{33}}}

\displaystyle\llbracket{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\textsf{iter}\{{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}e_{1}}\mid{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}y}\hookrightarrow{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}e_{2}}\}({\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}e_{3}})}\rrbracket({\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\vec{\sigma}})

\displaystyle\textsf{By compiling}

\displaystyle={\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\sum_{n\in\mathbb{N}}}\,\llbracket{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}e_{3}}}\rrbracket{((({\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\vec{\sigma_{1}}})_{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\Delta_{13}})_{{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\Delta_{23}}})}({\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}n})\,{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\cdot}\,\llbracket{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}e_{2}}}\rrbracket{((({\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\vec{\sigma_{1}}})_{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\Delta_{12}})_{{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\Delta_{22}}})}^{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}n}(\llbracket{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}e_{1}}}\rrbracket{((({\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\vec{\sigma_{1}}})_{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\Delta_{11}})_{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\Delta_{31}}}))

\displaystyle\textsf{By induction}

\displaystyle={\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\sum_{n\in\mathbb{N}}}\,\llbracket{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}e_{3}}}\rrbracket{(({\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\vec{\sigma_{1}}})_{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\Delta_{23}})}({\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}n})\,{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\cdot}\,\llbracket{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}e_{2}}}\rrbracket{(({\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\vec{\sigma}}_{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\Delta_{12}})_{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\Delta_{22}})}^{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}n}(\llbracket{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}e_{1}}}\rrbracket{((({\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\vec{\sigma_{1}}})_{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\Delta_{11}})_{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\Delta_{31}}}))

\displaystyle\textsf{Because }(({\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\vec{\sigma_{1}}})_{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\Delta_{13}})_{{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\Delta_{23}}}=({\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\vec{\sigma_{1}}})_{{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\Delta_{23}}}

\displaystyle={\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\sum_{n\in\mathbb{N}}}\,\llbracket{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}e_{3}}}\rrbracket{(({\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\vec{\sigma_{1}}})_{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\Delta_{23}})}({\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}n})\,{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\cdot}\,\llbracket{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}e_{2}}}\rrbracket{(({\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\vec{\sigma_{1}}})_{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\Delta_{22}})}^{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}n}(\llbracket{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}e_{1}}}\rrbracket{((({\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\vec{\sigma_{1}}})_{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\Delta_{11}})_{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\Delta_{21}}}))

\displaystyle=\llbracket{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}e_{1}e_{2}}\rrbracket({\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\vec{\sigma_{1}}})_{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\Delta_{2}}

\displaystyle\textsf{By compiling}

∎

Appendix C
Experiments

The following experiments replicate data from prior work on differentiable programming with first-class conditionals. Since our specification and implementation differ, these data were helpful to establish consistency with prior work. They are binary classification tasks with explicit conditional structure. Our findings are consistent with prior work, showing that programming neural networks with first-class conditionals helps them solve these tasks. Detailed specifications of model parameters are available in the artifact; they are consistent with prior work.

C.1.
Conditional classification: XOR

C.1.1.
Task

Figure 18
.
XOR task

This experiment studies how neural networks learn a binary classification involving
XOR
logic, shown in Fig.
18
. The input is two images
x_{1}
and
x_{2}
:

\triangleright

If
x_{1}
is even and
x_{2}
is odd, return
✓

\triangleright

If
x_{1}
is odd and
x_{2}
is even, return
✓

\triangleright

Otherwise, return
✗

C.1.2.
Models

Model D
is a neural network programmed using
{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\textsf{Cajal}}{\scriptstyle({\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\multimap},\,{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\mathbb{2}},\,{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\mathbb{N}})}
, the
XOR
structure is
directly
programmed using our compiler.

\llbracket{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\textsf{if}\;\underline{x}\;\textsf{then}\;(\textsf{if}\;\underline{y}\;\textsf{then}\;\underline{{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\textsf{ff}}}\;\textsf{else}\;\underline{{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\textsf{tt}}})\;\textsf{else}\;(\textsf{if}\;\underline{y}\;\textsf{then}\;\underline{{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\textsf{tt}}}\;\textsf{else}\;\underline{{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}\textsf{ff}}})}\rrbracket({\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\vec{x}},{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}\vec{y}})

Model T
replaces this
{\color[rgb]{0,0.329,0.576}2}
-linear map with random matrix
{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0.14}W}
, similar to our iterative image transform experiments.
Model I
is a simple 3-layer feedforward neural network.
